**项目核心**：FREIGE = 用 CED (Contrastive Evidence Discrimination) reward 训练 LLM 做文档级关系抽取 + 可验证证据生成。

**关键发现链**：
- D008: QLoRA 4-bit + kl_coef 不兼容（KL 爆炸），kl_coef=0.01 是折中
- D024: **CED reward 有 anti-mode-collapse 作用**（论文核心 finding）——同配置下 CED 成功、extraction-only/flat-NLI 都 collapse
- D027/D029: lr=5e-5 全灭（灾难性遗忘），转 lr=5e-6
- D032: CED 有 recall shortcut（少预测→高 CED），已部署 --ced_recall_penalty fix

**SFT baseline**：F1=0.4057, Evi-F1=0.7979, EDCR=0.7118

**MVP-2 判定标准**：
- 核心：(CED_Evi-F1 − SFT_Evi-F1) ≥ 2pp
- F1 不退化：CED_F1 ≥ max(extraction-only_F1, SFT_F1) − 5pp
- 当前 gate：P0 ckpt-20 rel_f1 > 0.30（不灾难性遗忘）

## 项目硬约束 (memory.md 节选)

## 环境与兼容性

- **AutoDL HuggingFace 下载不可靠**：大模型（如 Qwen3-4B）从 HF 下载反复 ReadTimeout。解决方案：用 ModelScope (`from modelscope import snapshot_download`) 下载到本地缓存，再用本地路径加载。(2026-05-14)
- **vLLM 版本不兼容 TRL**：服务器预装 vLLM 0.18.1，但 TRL 支持范围为 0.11.0-0.17.1。MVP-2 若用 TRL 内部 vLLM rollout 可能有兼容问题，需提前测试。(2026-05-14)
- **flash_attn 未安装**：rtx5090-4x 服务器未预装 flash-attn，训练脚本需用 `attn_implementation="sdpa"` 替代。(2026-05-14)
- **Qwen3-4B 本地路径**：已通过 ModelScope 下载到 `/root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen3-4B`（7.6GB）。后续训练脚本应指向此路径而非 HF model ID。(2026-05-14)
- **确认训练配置**：ZeRO-2 + QLoRA (4-bit NF4, rank 64, alpha 128) + SDPA。ZeRO-3 + QLoRA 不兼容（uint8 vs all_gather dtype）。(2026-05-14)
- **Smoke test 假阴性**：synthetic data reward variance=0 导致零梯度是数学必然（format reward 对所有 completions 输出相同分数），不是训练 bug。评估 smoke test 时只看兼容性和显存，不看 loss 趋势。(2026-05-14)
- **TRL 1.1.0 API 变更（关键）**：服务器 TRL 版本 1.1.0，与旧版有 3 个破坏性变更：(1) `SFTConfig.max_seq_length` → `max_length`；(2) `SFTTrainer(tokenizer=...)` → `SFTTrainer(processing_class=...)`；(3) 多 GPU 需要 accelerate `MULTI_GPU` + `device_map={"": local_rank}`。**grpo_trainer.py 也用了 `tokenizer=`，GRPO 提交前必须改为 `processing_class=`**。(2026-05-14)
- **rtx5090-4x 和 gpu-5090-4x-1 是同一台机器**：两个 SSH endpoint 指向同一台 4×RTX 5090 32GB 服务器。(2026-05-14)
- **wandb 配置**：entity=`xiaoyucorynne-`，GRPO 项目名=`freige-grpo`（但截至 2026-05-14 项目未成功创建——训练脚本可能未连接 wandb）。API key 存于 `~/.netrc`。MCP 工具 `list_wandb_runs` 有内部 bug（`wandb_project` 参数传递错误），需直接用 `fetch_wandb_run_history` + 已知 run_id。(2026-05-14)

## 可查的历史
前 18 轮 handoff 在 `task_context/history/agent/loop_*.md`，如有疑问可 Read 回查。
完整聊天归档可用 `recall_session_history("关键词")` 搜索。

## 第一个动作要求
1. 用 `read_registry` 确认当前 RUNNING/PREPARING 实验状态（防止幻觉记忆过时状态）
2. 回复一句话确认你看到了上面的硬约束 + 当前活跃实验，再开始任何工具调用

    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

QLoRA quantization noise makes ref/policy log-prob divergence unreliable, causing KL values to reach 10^8-10^11. Same root cause as r3 and flatnli. Fix: kl_coef=0.0 in r2 retries.\\\"}, \\\"mvp2_grpo_flatnli\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T07:10:33.848Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T12:13:34.747Z\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"Flat-NLI reward (non-contrastive) improves Evi-F1 over extraction-only but less than CED — validates contrastive component's incremental value\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"failed\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"KL divergence explosion at step 5 due to kl_coef=0.05 + QLoRA 4-bit incompatibility (D008). QLoRA quantization noise makes ref/policy log-prob divergence unreliable. Same root cause as r3 and extract. Fix: kl_coef=0.0 in r2 retries.\\\"}, \\\"mvp2_grpo_ced_r3\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T11:47:53.099Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T12:09:23.005Z\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"CED reward with balanced weights (format×0.3, f1×3.0, ced×1.0) + stable training (num_iterations=1, KL early stop at 100) + kl_coef=0.05 improves Evi-F1 over SFT baseline\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"failed\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"KL divergence explosion at step 5: first pass KL=4.4M, second pass KL=69.5B (threshold=100). KL early stopping correctly triggered, model saved but no useful training occurred.\\\\n\\\\nRoot cause: single-GPU training without --gradient_accumulation_steps (effective batch=1) → extremely noisy gradients → policy diverged from reference in 5 steps. Combined with QLoRA merge rounding amplifying initial policy-reference gap.\\\\n\\\\nKey metrics at step 5: loss=269987, kl=4.4M, entropy=0.084 (near 0), format_reward=0.10, f1_reward=0.05, ced_reward=0.15, grad_norm=0.16. EXIT_CODE=129 is artifact of KL early stop exit path.\\\\n\\\\nerror_type: hyperparameter | can_auto_fix: false\\\\nFix options: (1) add --gradient_accumulation_steps 16 for effective batch ~16; (2) reduce lr from 5e-5 to 5e-6; (3) increase kl_coef from 0.05 to 0.5; (4) lr warmup steps\\\"}, \\\"mvp2_grpo_ced_r2\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T10:58:11.663Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T11:31:49.724Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"killed\\\"}, \\\"mvp2_grpo_ced_r1\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T08:02:41.858Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T10:37:23.856Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"killed\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"Mode collapse diagnosed at step 80/189 (F1=0.001, format reward hacking). Root causes and fixes applied to grpo_trainer.py:\\\\n\\\\n(A) Checkpoint: save_strategy was \\\\\\\"epoch\\\\\\\" → now supports --save_steps/--save_total_limit CLI args, defaults to steps-based saving. Validated: checkpoint-1/2 saved in dry-run.\\\\n\\\\n(B) Reward weighting: all 3 rewards (format/f1/ced) were equally summed by TRL → added WeightedRewardFn wrapper + --format_reward_weight/--f1_reward_weight/--ced_reward_weight CLI args. Dry-run confirmed weighting works (format raw=0.1 × w=0.3 → 0.03).\\\\n\\\\n(C) Clip ratio: num_iterations=1 (default) meant single pass, ratio≡1, clipping never activated → added --num_iterations CLI arg (set to 2 in r2). Also added --max_steps, --kl_coef (beta). Dry-run clip_ratio=0 expected (zero reward variance → zero advantage → no update needed).\\\\n\\\\n(D) Added --save_total_limit CLI arg (previously hardcoded to 2, now configurable).\\\\n\\\\nDry-run validated: 2 steps completed, checkpoints saved, no OOM with num_iterations=2 on 4xRTX5090. completions/clipped_ratio=1.0 (all hit max_completion_length=1024) — expected for cold-start without warmup.\\\"}, \\\"mvp2_grpo_ced\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T07:10:31.165Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T08:00:02.874Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": _source line 56 · 2026-05-14T21:24:24.229Z_

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_019YKY4E3h8QgAgdcbuWjRaG`</summary>

```text
1	---
2	name: resource-saturation
3	description: "当你准备向 Director 汇报前、收到 cron/worker 回调、审批通过准备开跑、怀疑 Worker 池不够饱和时,立即使用本 skill。四部分:A) GPU 分配率(分配了没跑=失职) B) GPU 效率 C) 非 GPU 任务并行(Worker 池<2=失职) D) 汇报必须带 [资源] 行。"
4	always: false
5	roles:
6	  - director
7	  - agent
8	---
9	
10	# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）
11	
12	## A. GPU 分配率检查
13	
14	调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:
15	- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号
16	- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）
17	- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️
18	
19	任何"分配了但没跑"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:
20	- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备
21	- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）
22	- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报"暂无可铺实验"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作
23	
24	**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。
25	
26	## B. GPU 效率检查
27	
28	调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:
29	- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务
30	- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）
31	- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优
32	
33	## C. 非 GPU 任务并行
34	
35	GPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:
36	- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint
37	- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置
38	- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索
39	- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合
40	- 论文章节起草
41	- 代码 review / refactor
42

GPU 分配率检查\n\n调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:\n- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号\n- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）\n- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️\n\n任何\"分配了但没跑\"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:\n- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备\n- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）\n- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报\"暂无可铺实验\"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作\n\n**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。\n\n## B. GPU 效率检查\n\n调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:\n- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务\n- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）\n- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优\n\n## C. 非 GPU 任务并行\n\nGPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:\n- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint\n- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置\n- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索\n- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合\n- 论文章节起草\n- 代码 review / refactor\n\n**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。\n\n## D. 汇报格式\n\n向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:\n\n    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)\n",
